# In pursuance of sub-Section (3) of Section 19 of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the list of Members of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India as on the 1st day of April 1955, is hereby published for general information.

NOTIFICATION New Delhi—1, the 14th September 1955 No. 10-CA(l)/54.—In pursuance of sub-Section

(3) of Section 19 of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the list of Members of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India as on the 1st day of April 1955, is hereby published for general information.

Explanatory Notes There are two classes of members, viz., Fellows and Associates.

They are entitled to use the letters "F.C.A." and "A.C.A." respectively after their names. 'F' after a member's name denotes that he is a Fellow and 'A' after a member's name denotes that he is an Associate. The date in brackets after 'F' indicates the date of admission to fellowship and the date in brackets after 'A' indicates the date of enrolment as Associate. The number in brackets at the end of an entry Indicates Membership Number.

A dagger( ) before a member's name indicates that the member is not in practice.

A double-dagger () before a member's name indicates that he has been enrolled under the proviso to clause (v) of sub-Section (1) of Section 4 of the Act. The names of such members have been included in the topographical section only with a view to give a complete list of members.

Such members will, however, be governed by the conditions laid down by the Resolution of the Council under the aforesaid clause.

The names of firms of Chartered Accountants are given in italics. The names of the partners of a firm are shown in brackets after its name.

Members who are employed by a firm of Chartered Accountants are described as 'with' that firm.

Members who are employed by a concern other than a firm of Chartered Accountants are described as 'C/o' that concern.

The first part of the list has been arranged alphabetically and the second part of the list has been arranged topographically according to Regions. The names in each region have also been arranged alphabetically.
